# UX issues

The UX loop maintains this file (`/ux`). There is one line per issue, and the line moves to the section for its current status.

- **Line format:** ID · severity (S1–S4; P0–P2 for UX_REVIEW items) · area · status · title · main `path:line` · the run that last changed it, then a short note.
- **Areas:**
  - A — voice capture;
  - B — calendar, home and tasks;
  - C — lists, notes, groups and sharing;
  - D — first run, sign-in, settings, notifications, native surfaces and theme.
- **Notes:** P# and D# are the packages and decisions in that run's `plan.md`. "Merged" lists the duplicates folded into a line.

## Open
- **U2** · P0 · A · open · "Finished" means two things; automatic stops park the words · `App/lib/screens/voice_task_screen.dart:846` · 2026-10-28-app · verified 2026-10-13 (b2 round 1, P35; D3). "Listo" always stops and processes. Cancel, Back and a swipe down ask "¿Conservar lo que has dicho?", only when there are words. Tests: `native_speech_test.dart:993-1138`. Its regressions UX-440, UX-441 and UX-444 are closed. 2026-10-18-commands (review A): holds; its question misleads on deletions (UX-903, P128); on a result with nothing to add, Cancel will just close (UX-917: D1 answered 2026-10-18 night; P152). 2026-10-19 (b4 round 1, P152): UX-917 verified in code; a result with nothing to add closes at once, and one with a task keeps the question. 2026-10-19-app (review A): holds; at the 60 s limit with nothing heard, the on-device path resets silently (UX-1304, P167). 2026-10-28-app (review A1): regressed: a first capture's Cancelar, Back or swipe during «Entendiendo…» parks the words without asking (UX-2102, P273)

- **UX-511** · S2 · B · device-check · After an upload, task screen blinks and jumps to top · `App/lib/widgets/attachment_panel.dart:218` · 2026-10-13-app · regression R2B-1 from P95 (b7 round 2). Partly fixed in b7 round 3 (P97; reviewer: partly, failed 1 time): `taskRepositoryProvider` and `captureOwnerProvider` now watch only `account?.id`, so an account update no longer turns Task details into a spinner or runs a second sync (tests: `account_update_test.dart`). Still failing: after every sync that ends with the task open, the scroll position moves; `account_update_test.dart:381-396` ("stays where the user scrolled") fails. Cause, outside P97's files: `attachmentEntriesProvider` (`attachment_panel.dart:25-29`) watches `taskProvider`, and `attachments.when(...)` at :218 (default `skipLoadingOnReload: false`) swaps the file cards for a 4 dp progress bar on each reload, so a user at the bottom of a long task is pushed up by about one card per file. Reviewer's fix: pass `skipLoadingOnReload: true` at :218; `_busy`'s bar at :208 already shows the user's own progress; no copy change. Not re-planned: b7 ended after round 3. Phone: on a free account, open a long task, scroll to the bottom, attach a photo; the card never disappears and the view doesn't move Fixed in code 2026-10-14 by the main session, after b7, with the reviewer's one-line fix: `skipLoadingOnReload: true` on `attachments.when(...)` in `attachment_panel.dart`. The failing test `account_update_test.dart` ('stays where the user scrolled') now passes. Not re-verified by a reviewer. Phone: on a free account, open a long task, scroll to the bottom and attach a photo; the card never disappears and the view stays put.

Still open, cause server-side: `Backend/core/serializers.py` (~:295) and `Backend/core/ai.py:88-112` don't use it yet, for the main session. History: planned in P259 (plan 3). Round 1 (2026-10-23, verify-p3-r1-B): partly fixed, failed once. Fixed on screen (no tap, chevron or «Activar recordatorios»; «Recordar antes de» and the needs-day line hidden; the chip at full contrast). Still failing: the capture card's collapsed row keeps `enabled: onTap != null` (`App/lib/widgets/capture_review_editor.dart:418-419`), so Marta's line with no tap is a node with an enabled state that is off, and TalkBack would say «Recordatorio para Marta, desactivado». Replanned in P264 (plan 3, round 2): `button: line.onTap != null`, `enabled: line.onTap == null ? null : widget.enabled`; the test asserts no enabled state. History: Filed by the organizer: a tap on «Recordatorio para Ana» (chip, form row, card row) opens the one-tap list, «Recordar antes de» shows under a deadline (`App/lib/widgets/task_planning_control.dart:165`), and the card asks «Para recordártelo, ponle un día.»; any choice saves this account's own value, which the engine never rings here (`ReminderBlock.notForAccount`) and Ana never gets. The contract («Shared tasks»): no choices on a task given to someone else. Fix: no tap, chevron or «Activar recordatorios»; no «Recordar antes de»; no needs-day line; a chip with no tap drawn at full contrast. Severity set by the organizer

Filed by the organizer: since every reminder is personal, a «lector» in a shared project gets «Todos» tasks' reminders with their own defaults but can choose none: the chip has no tap without edit rights, and the server refuses a reader's change to a task, their own reminders included (`Backend/core/list_access.py:75`). The owner's D6: "everyone should be able to set their own pattern for reminder frequency". Round 1 had failed once (P271). Tests: `App/test/` sharing_sync_test.dart, task_detail_test.dart. After a 409 the banner lists each new or changed handover (kind, name, recipient), bold in the list until the next tap; general line during the reload or when nothing is new; the password stays. Its TalkBack reading was UX-817 (P125). Tests: `App/test/delete_account_test.dart:2481-2561`, `:3107` (Manrope, 2× on 320 dp)
- **UX-809** · S2 · D · verified · «Inténtalo más tarde» hides a 15-minute lock · `App/lib/screens/delete_account_screen.dart:181` · 2026-10-15-deletion · verified 2026-10-15 (b1 round 1, P121); re-verified 2026-10-16 (b2 round 1, P126; reviewer: fixed, verify-b2-r1-D). «Espera N minutos», rounded up, at least 1. The wait now comes first from the `Retry-After` header (`App/lib/services/api_client.dart:470-480`, whole seconds; DRF sends 900 with the server's `Throttled(wait=900)`), then the English detail text, else the old line (`delete_account_screen.dart:182-191`), so a Spanish detail still gives «Espera 15 minutos». Tests: `App/test/delete_account_test.dart:2056`, `:2081` (Catalan, 2× on 320 dp), `:3235-3370` (real ApiClient over MockClient; header beats text; no header gives the generic line). Sign-in's own «Espera un poco» isn't in this run

Review A: renaming the one primary button each month costs recognition. Review B keeps «Nueva tarea», which matches the form's page. Recommendation: keep «Nueva tarea»; otherwise `tasksEmptyForm` and `inboxEmptyForm` change too. Severity set by the organizer. - **UX-1211** · S1 · D · wontfix · No «Abrir el correo» button on the confirm screen · `App/lib/screens/verify_email_screen.dart:418` · 2026-10-18-commands · D10, review D's question 2: open the phone's email app from the screen? It adds a control, and Gmail's notification already shows the code in its subject. Recommendation: not now; watch for it in the §7 test with an email sign-up. Severity set by the organizer. Decided (D10, 2026-10-18 night, for the owner): not now; no change
- **UX-912** · S2 · A · wontfix · Plain dictation's wait with commands on is unmeasured · `App/lib/services/capture_processor.dart:229` · 2026-10-18-commands · D11, with UX-616: every dictation now asks for commands. Measure on staging (main session), same phone and network: 10+ one-task dictations with the flag and 10+ without, `extraction_ms` p50 and p95 in Diagnóstico de captura. If the flag adds 300 ms or more at p50, the cause is server-side (a longer prompt): decide it with UX-616. Inferred, not measured. Decided (D11, 2026-10-18 night): measured locally on 2026-10-18 instead of on staging, with the real free model (`gemma-4-26b-a4b-it`), 12 one-task Spanish dictations each way, server extraction time only: median 3.5 s with the commands flag, 4.3 s without (p95 3.8 s and 4.6 s), one task each and no false commands. The flag stays; no change
- **UX-616** · S2 · A · wontfix · The assistant's wait (about 5 s) is capture's longest · `App/lib/services/capture_processor.dart:207` · 2026-10-18-commands · review A's question 2: cut it on the server, with a faster model or by understanding while the person speaks? Staging: 1.3, 4.8, 4.8, 4.8 and 20.5 s. A cost decision; the app's share is UX-610 (P101). Severity set by the organizer. 2026-10-18-commands: UX-912 (D11) asks to measure, with it, what asking for commands adds to every dictation's wait. Decided 2026-10-18 (night) for the owner; cause server-side: settings only, `AI_ROUTE=auto` with `AI_FREE_ATTEMPT_SECONDS=10` on staging and production, so a slow free call falls back to the paid model sooner; it applies at the next deploy, which stays the owner's. D11 (UX-912) found the commands flag adds no wait. The app's share stays UX-610 (device check)
